#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}

+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}

+// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
+// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width);
+                        if (bad != width) failed.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}

+// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
+// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel,diagonal,rows,width) shared(failed) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])

+                // path. Dependencies permit later panels to start before unrelated

+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}

#include <cassert>

bool reference(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n) {
    for (size_t i=0; i<n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j=0; j<=i; ++j) {
            double sum=0;
            for (size_t k=0; k<j; ++k) sum += a[i*n+k]*a[j*n+k];
            if (i==j) {
                double v=a[i*n+i]-sum;
                if (v<=0) return false;
                a[i*n+i]=std::sqrt(v);
            } else a[i*n+j]=(a[i*n+j]-sum)/a[j*n+j];
        }
        for (size_t j=i+1; j<n; ++j) a[i*n+j]=0;
    }
    return true;
}

    for(size_t n : {size_t(0),size_t(1),size_t(3),size_t(63),size_t(64),size_t(65),size_t(129),size_t(513),size_t(1025)}) {
        std::vector<double> input(n*n);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(input,n);
        auto expected=input;
        assert(reference(expected,n));
        for (int threads : {1,2,8}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            auto actual=input;
            assert(choleskyDecomposition(actual,n));
            double maxerror=0;
            for(size_t i=0;i<n*n;++i) {
                assert(std::isfinite(actual[i]));
                maxerror=std::max(maxerror,std::abs(expected[i]-actual[i]));
            }
            assert(maxerror<1e-10);
            for(size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for(size_t j=i+1;j<n;++j) assert(actual[i*n+j]==0);
            printf(\"n=%zu, threads=%d: reference maximum difference %.3e\\n\",n,threads,maxerror);
        }
    }
    for(int threads : {1,8}) {
        omp_set_num_threads(threads);
        for(size_t bad : {size_t(0),size_t(63),size_t(64),size_t(129)}) {
            size_t n=130;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;

+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;

 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <omp.h>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}

+// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
+// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel,diagonal,rows,width) shared(failed) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, n, rows, width);

-            } else {

-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {

+                // Queue the next diagonal's updates first to shorten the critical
+                // path. Dependencies permit later panels to start before unrelated
+                // trailing updates finish; there is no barrier per panel.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* target = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(target,left,right,rows,cols,width) shared(failed) depend(in:left[0],right[0]) depend(inout:target[0])

codex
The first version passes validation across thread counts, including sizes that cross tile boundaries. It also agrees with the original factorization within about \(1.4\times10^{-14}\) on the reference checks so far. At size 2048, factorization drops from 456 ms with one thread to 26 ms with 32 threads. I’m refining the update kernel to improve throughput further.

 #include <cstring>

+#include <omp.h>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// The unused upper triangle holds transposed panels until factorization ends.
+// This permits contiguous SIMD loads across output columns without packing each
+// update or allocating another matrix. Four rows by eight columns fit in SIMD
+// registers and retain their accumulators for the entire inner product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* YT, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * n + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * n + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * n + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, double* PT, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) PT[j * n + i] = P[i * n + j];
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// The unused upper triangle holds transposed panels until factorization ends.
+// This permits contiguous SIMD loads across output columns without packing each
+// update or allocating another matrix. Four rows by eight columns fit in SIMD
+// registers and retain their accumulators for the entire inner product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* YT, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * n + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * n + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * n + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, double* PT, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) PT[j * n + i] = P[i * n + j];
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;

+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;

+        }

+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// The unused upper triangle holds transposed panels until factorization ends.
+// This permits contiguous SIMD loads across output columns without packing each
+// update or allocating another matrix. Four rows by eight columns fit in SIMD
+// registers and retain their accumulators for the entire inner product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* YT, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * n + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * n + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * n + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, double* PT, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) PT[j * n + i] = P[i * n + j];
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

 #include <cstring>

+#include <omp.h>

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
