-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

namespace {
constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;

// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
template <bool subtract>
void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
            }
            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
            double* c1 = c0 + n;
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
            } else {
                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
            }
        }
        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
            const double* y = Y + j * n;
            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) {
                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
            } else {
                C[i * n + j] = s0;
                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
            }
        }
    }
    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
            }
            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
    }
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
        size_t j = 0;
        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
                }
            }
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
            }
        }
        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
            }
            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
        }
    }
    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
        }
    }
}

size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
        double* row = D + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
        }
        double sum = 0.0;
        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
        const double val = row[i] - sum;
        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
        row[i] = sqrt(val);
    }
    return width;
}

void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
        double* row = P + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
        }
    }
}
} // namespace

// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    double* data = A.data();
    std::atomic<size_t> failed(n);
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp single nowait
        {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal,k,width) shared(failed) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
                {
                    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width);
                        if (bad != width) failed.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                    }
                }
                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel,diagonal,rows,width) shared(failed) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
                    {
                        if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, n, rows, width);
                    }
                }
                // Queue the next diagonal's updates first to shorten the critical
                // path. Dependencies permit later panels to start before unrelated
                // trailing updates finish; there is no barrier per panel.
                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
                    const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
                        double* target = data + i * n + j;
                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(target,left,right,rows,cols,width) shared(failed) depend(in:left[0],right[0]) depend(inout:target[0])
                        {
                            if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
                                updateTile(target, left, right, n, rows, cols, width);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            #pragma omp taskwait
        }
        // Wait for the entire task graph before zeroing the upper triangle.
        #pragma omp barrier
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
        }
    }
    const size_t bad = failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (bad != n) {
        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Keep rand_r serial so every thread count uses the original seeded stream.
    // Only the lower triangle of the symmetric product needs to be computed.
    const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic,1)
        for (size_t ti = 0; ti < tiles; ++ti) {
            for (size_t tj = 0; tj < tiles; ++tj) {
                if (tj > ti) continue;
                const size_t i = ti * tileSize, j = tj * tileSize;
                productTile<false>(A.data() + i * n + j, B.data() + i * n,
                                   B.data() + j * n, n, std::min(tileSize, n - i),
                                   std::min(tileSize, n - j), n);
            }
        }
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) A[i * n + j] = A[j * n + i];
            A[i * n + i] += n;
        }
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Fuse the symmetric reconstruction and comparison. The known zero upper
    // triangle lets each dot product stop at its last nonzero element.
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;

    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            const double lower = A_orig[i * n + j], upper = A_orig[j * n + i];
            const double error = fabs(sum - lower), upperError = fabs(sum - upper);
            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, upperError));
            relError = std::max(relError, std::max(error / (fabs(lower) + 1e-10),
                                                 upperError / (fabs(upper) + 1e-10)));
        }
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('cholesky/cholesky.cpp')
s=p.read_text().replace('constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;', '''constexpr size_t maxTileSize = 128;

size_t chooseTileSize(size_t n) {
    // Small tiles expose work on small matrices; larger tiles amortize task and
    // packing costs as the matrix grows.
    return n <= 1024 ? 64 : (n <= 2048 ? 96 : 128);
}

int chooseThreadCount(size_t n, size_t tileSize) {
    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize "'!= 0);
    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(),
                                           std::max<size_t>(1, 2 * tiles)));
}'"''')

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

The dependency checks pass, including rejection of non-positive-definite matrices. Performance measurements favor smaller tiles for small matrices and larger tiles for large matrices, so I’m adding adaptive tile sizing. I’m also limiting idle threads on small inputs and fixing the timing calculation so sub-millisecond runs report finite GFLOPS.

s=s[:a]+f+s[b:]

                // matrix rows with a large, potentially aliasing cache stride.
                constexpr size_t stride = maxTileSize + 8;
                alignas(64) double target[maxTileSize * stride] = {};
                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// C = X * Y^T, or C -= X * Y^T. All matrices have row stride n.
+// A 2-by-4 register block reuses inputs across eight dot products. Vectorizing
+// their contiguous reduction dimension avoids strided gathers in row-major data.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                 size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 1 < rows; i += 2) {
+        const double* x0 = X + i * n;
+        const double* x1 = x0 + n;
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 3 < cols; j += 4) {
+            const double* y0 = Y + j * n;
+            const double* y1 = y0 + n;
+            const double* y2 = y1 + n;
+            const double* y3 = y2 + n;
+            double s00 = 0.0, s01 = 0.0, s02 = 0.0, s03 = 0.0;
+            double s10 = 0.0, s11 = 0.0, s12 = 0.0, s13 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s00,s01,s02,s03,s10,s11,s12,s13)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s00 += x0[k] * y0[k];
+                s01 += x0[k] * y1[k];
+                s02 += x0[k] * y2[k];
+                s03 += x0[k] * y3[k];
+                s10 += x1[k] * y0[k];
+                s11 += x1[k] * y1[k];
+                s12 += x1[k] * y2[k];
+                s13 += x1[k] * y3[k];
+            }
+            double* c0 = C + i * n + j;
+            double* c1 = c0 + n;
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                c0[0] -= s00; c0[1] -= s01; c0[2] -= s02; c0[3] -= s03;
+                c1[0] -= s10; c1[1] -= s11; c1[2] -= s12; c1[3] -= s13;
+            } else {
+                c0[0] = s00; c0[1] = s01; c0[2] = s02; c0[3] = s03;
+                c1[0] = s10; c1[1] = s11; c1[2] = s12; c1[3] = s13;
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            const double* y = Y + j * n;
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                s0 += x0[k] * y[k];
+                s1 += x1[k] * y[k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            } else {
+                C[i * n + j] = s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j] = s1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                sum += X[i * n + k] * Y[j * n + k];
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * n + j] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+void updateTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = tileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[tileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < cols; j += 8) {
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double x0 = X[i * n + k], x1 = X[(i + 1) * n + k];
+                const double x2 = X[(i + 2) * n + k], x3 = X[(i + 3) * n + k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double y = YT[k * pitch + j + c];
+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;
+                    s2[c] += x2 * y;
+                    s3[c] += x3 * y;
+                }
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                C[i * n + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                C[(i + 1) * n + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                C[(i + 2) * n + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                C[(i + 3) * n + j + c] -= s3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double s0 = 0.0, s1 = 0.0, s2 = 0.0, s3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s0,s1,s2,s3)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                const double y = YT[k * pitch + j];
+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            C[i * n + j] -= s0;
+            C[(i + 1) * n + j] -= s1;
+            C[(i + 2) * n + j] -= s2;
+            C[(i + 3) * n + j] -= s3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            C[i * n + j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace

+    // Small tiles expose work on small matrices; larger tiles amortize task and
+    // packing costs as the matrix grows.
+    return n <= 1024 ? 64 : (n <= 2048 ? 96 : 128);

+int chooseThreadCount(size_t n, size_t tileSize) {
+    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize != 0);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                           std::max<size_t>(1, 2 * tiles)));

+                s0 += X[i * n + k] * y;
+                s1 += X[(i + 1) * n + k] * y;
+                s2 += X[(i + 2) * n + k] * y;
+                s3 += X[(i + 3) * n + k] * y;
+            }
+            if constexpr (subtract) {
+                C[i * cStride + j] -= s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * cStride + j] -= s1;
+                C[(i + 2) * cStride + j] -= s2;
+                C[(i + 3) * cStride + j] -= s3;
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                C[i * cStride + j] += s0;
+                C[(i + 1) * cStride + j] += s1;
+                C[(i + 2) * cStride + j] += s2;
+                C[(i + 3) * cStride + j] += s3;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) sum += X[i * n + k] * YT[k * pitch + j];
+            if constexpr (subtract) C[i * cStride + j] -= sum;
+            else C[i * cStride + j] += sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}
+
+// D stores the diagonal tile transposed and its diagonal reciprocals. Updating
+// four right-hand sides together vectorizes across contiguous panel columns,
+// including the short inner products near the start of the triangular solve.
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width,
+                size_t stride) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        double* p0 = P + i * n;
+        double* p1 = p0 + n;
+        double* p2 = p1 + n;
+        double* p3 = p2 + n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            const double* d = D + j * stride;
+            const double x0 = p0[j] * d[j], x1 = p1[j] * d[j];
+            const double x2 = p2[j] * d[j], x3 = p3[j] * d[j];
+            p0[j] = x0; p1[j] = x1; p2[j] = x2; p3[j] = x3;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t k = j + 1; k < width; ++k) {
+                p0[k] -= x0 * d[k];
+                p1[k] -= x1 * d[k];
+                p2[k] -= x2 * d[k];
+                p3[k] -= x3 * d[k];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            const double* d = D + j * stride;
+            const double x = row[j] * d[j];
+            row[j] = x;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t k = j + 1; k < width; ++k) row[k] -= x * d[k];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
+// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
+// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileSize = chooseTileSize(n);
+    const int threads = chooseThreadCount(n, tileSize);
+    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize != 0);
+    const size_t diagonalStride = tileSize + 8;
+    // Shared packed diagonals occupy O(n * tileSize) space and are read-only
+    // after their factor task. The matrix-tile dependency also protects these.
+    std::vector<double> packedDiagonals(tiles * tileSize * diagonalStride);
+    double* packed = packedDiagonals.data();
+    std::atomic<size_t> failed(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single nowait
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                double* packedDiagonal = packed + (k / tileSize) * tileSize * diagonalStride;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal,packedDiagonal,k,width) shared(failed) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width);
+                        if (bad != width) {
+                            failed.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        } else {
+                            for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                                packedDiagonal[j * diagonalStride + j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * n + j];
+                                for (size_t i = j + 1; i < width; ++i)
+                                    packedDiagonal[j * diagonalStride + i] = diagonal[i * n + j];
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel,diagonal,packedDiagonal,rows,width) shared(failed) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
+                            solvePanel(panel, packedDiagonal, n, rows, width, diagonalStride);
+                    }
+                }
+                // Queue the next diagonal's updates first to shorten the critical
+                // path. Dependencies permit later panels to start before unrelated
+                // trailing updates finish; there is no barrier per panel.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* target = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(target,left,right,rows,cols,width) shared(failed) depend(in:left[0],right[0]) depend(inout:target[0])
+                        {
+                            if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
+                                productTile<true>(target, left, right, n, rows, cols, width, n);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        // Wait for the entire task graph before zeroing the upper triangle.
+        #pragma omp barrier
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
+    const size_t bad = failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    if (bad != n) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

+size_t chooseTileSize(size_t n) {
+    // Small tiles expose work on small matrices; larger tiles amortize task and
+    // packing costs as the matrix grows.
+    return n <= 1024 ? 64 : (n <= 2048 ? 96 : 128);

+int chooseThreadCount(size_t n, size_t tileSize) {
+    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize != 0);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                           std::max<size_t>(1, 2 * tiles)));
+}

+// Pack one input tile transposed so SIMD loads across output columns are
+// contiguous. Padding avoids cache-set conflicts for power-of-two matrix sizes.
+// Four rows by eight columns retain their sums in registers through the product.
+template <bool subtract>
+void productTile(double* C, const double* X, const double* Y, size_t n,
+                size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t depth, size_t cStride) {
+    constexpr size_t pitch = maxTileSize + 8;
+    alignas(64) double YT[maxTileSize * pitch];
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) YT[k * pitch + j] = Y[j * n + k];
+    }
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;

+                    s0[c] += x0 * y;
+                    s1[c] += x1 * y;

-                    return false;
+            }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                if constexpr (subtract) {
+                    C[i * cStride + j + c] -= s0[c];
+                    C[(i + 1) * cStride + j + c] -= s1[c];
+                    C[(i + 2) * cStride + j + c] -= s2[c];
+                    C[(i + 3) * cStride + j + c] -= s3[c];
+                } else {
+                    C[i * cStride + j + c] += s0[c];
+                    C[(i + 1) * cStride + j + c] += s1[c];

+size_t factorDiagonal(double* D, size_t n, size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        double* row = D + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) sum += row[k] * D[j * n + k];
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / D[j * n + j];
+        }
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) sum += row[k] * row[k];
+        const double val = row[i] - sum;
+        if (val <= 0.0) return i;
+        row[i] = sqrt(val);
+    }
+    return width;
+}

+// D stores the diagonal tile transposed and its diagonal reciprocals. Updating
+// four right-hand sides together vectorizes across contiguous panel columns,
+// including the short inner products near the start of the triangular solve.
+void solvePanel(double* P, const double* D, size_t n, size_t rows, size_t width,
+                size_t stride) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 3 < rows; i += 4) {
+        double* p0 = P + i * n;
+        double* p1 = p0 + n;
+        double* p2 = p1 + n;
+        double* p3 = p2 + n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            const double* d = D + j * stride;
+            const double x0 = p0[j] * d[j], x1 = p1[j] * d[j];
+            const double x2 = p2[j] * d[j], x3 = p3[j] * d[j];
+            p0[j] = x0; p1[j] = x1; p2[j] = x2; p3[j] = x3;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t k = j + 1; k < width; ++k) {
+                p0[k] -= x0 * d[k];
+                p1[k] -= x1 * d[k];
+                p2[k] -= x2 * d[k];
+                p3[k] -= x3 * d[k];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double* row = P + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            const double* d = D + j * stride;
+            const double x = row[j] * d[j];
+            row[j] = x;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t k = j + 1; k < width; ++k) row[k] -= x * d[k];
+        }
+    }

+// In-place tiled Cholesky, A = L * L^T. A tile's first element is its
+// dependency token; tasks always access the entire corresponding tile.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileSize = chooseTileSize(n);
+    const int threads = chooseThreadCount(n, tileSize);
+    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize != 0);
+    const size_t diagonalStride = tileSize + 8;
+    // Shared packed diagonals occupy O(n * tileSize) space and are read-only
+    // after their factor task. The matrix-tile dependency also protects these.
+    std::vector<double> packedDiagonals(tiles * tileSize * diagonalStride);
+    double* packed = packedDiagonals.data();
+    std::atomic<size_t> failed(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single nowait
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                double* packedDiagonal = packed + (k / tileSize) * tileSize * diagonalStride;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal,packedDiagonal,k,width) shared(failed) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width);
+                        if (bad != width) {
+                            failed.store(k + bad, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        } else {
+                            for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                                packedDiagonal[j * diagonalStride + j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * n + j];
+                                for (size_t i = j + 1; i < width; ++i)
+                                    packedDiagonal[j * diagonalStride + i] = diagonal[i * n + j];
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel,diagonal,packedDiagonal,rows,width) shared(failed) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
+                            solvePanel(panel, packedDiagonal, n, rows, width, diagonalStride);
+                    }
+                }
+                // Queue the next diagonal's updates first to shorten the critical
+                // path. Dependencies permit later panels to start before unrelated
+                // trailing updates finish; there is no barrier per panel.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t cols = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* target = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(target,left,right,rows,cols,width) shared(failed) depend(in:left[0],right[0]) depend(inout:target[0])
+                        {
+                            if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == n)
+                                productTile<true>(target, left, right, n, rows, cols, width, n);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        // Wait for the entire task graph before zeroing the upper triangle.
+        #pragma omp barrier
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
+    const size_t bad = failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    if (bad != n) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;

    15	constexpr size_t maxTileSize = 128;

    17	size_t chooseTileSize(size_t n) {
    18	    // Small tiles expose work on small matrices; larger tiles amortize task and
    19	    // packing costs as the matrix grows.
    20	    return n <= 1024 ? 64 : (n <= 2048 ? 96 : 128);
    21	}
    22	
    23	int chooseThreadCount(size_t n, size_t tileSize) {
    24	    const size_t tiles = n / tileSize + (n % tileSize != 0);
    25	    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(),
    26	                                           std::max<size_t>(1, 2 * tiles)));
    27	}
